Not a mechanical refactor.  **RECLASSIFIED 2026-07-18: DESIGN (needs Nova ruling), not a defect-fix** — TD-082 deliberately left the nav-assert precedence un-unified (the one behavior-diverging piece); both sites importing assertionHelpers ≠ shared precedence. Reconcile only under a Nova design review. |
| TD-096 | evals compile gate is local-only — CI does not run `npm run check` (`check:core` + `tsc -p evals/tsconfig.json`). A type error in `evals/` only fails locally, not in CI. The unit-test gate (`test:unit`) IS in CI but the compile gate is not. Principle violated: local and CI gates must be as identical as possible — a gate that only exists locally is a gate that does not exist. Surfaced during the TD-085 push (run 28723077110): confirmed no `tsc`/`check` step in `.github/workflows/`. | Low · NB | Add `npm run check` as a CI workflow step in the test job (after `npm ci`, before or alongside the unit-test gate). Same pattern as wiring `test:unit` in TD-082. Small workflow change, own scoped commit, no other dependencies. |
| TD-097 | Audit + enforce no-hardcoded-paths across the entire codebase. FORGE must be portable across Windows/Mac/Linux/server. All paths must use runtime resolution (`__dirname`, `process.cwd()`, `path.resolve()`, `path.join()`) — never string-concatenated absolute paths or OS-specific separators. Each app's data (models, generated specs, configs) must be stored under paths derived from the repo root at runtime, not hardcoded locations. Principle: "would this break if someone cloned the repo on a Mac into `/Users/jane/projects/forge-framework`?" If yes, it's a defect. | Medium | Audit-first: grep the codebase for hardcoded path separators (`\\`, `C:\\`, `/home/`, `/Users/`) and absolute paths not using `path.join`/`__dirname`/`process.cwd()`. Fix any found. Enforce via code review on all new files. Apply to Bootstrap Mode and all future capabilities. |
| TD-098 | FORGE installation + setup documentation for all target platforms. When FORGE approaches commercial readiness it needs documented, tested setup steps for: (a) Windows (PowerShell, Node/npm, Playwright browser install, env setup); (b) Mac (Homebrew/nvm, same); (c) Linux/Ubuntu (apt, same); (d) Server/Docker (containerized setup, CI integration, headless Chromium). Currently there are no installation docs — a new user on any platform has no guided path to get FORGE running. | Low | Deferred until FORGE approaches commercial readiness (post-Dashboard, post-agentic-crawl). At that point, create an INSTALL.md + platform-specific setup scripts. Consider a Docker image for zero-dependency server deployment. |
| TD-100 | Mobile and IoT execution environments. FORGE's agent architecture (TD-013) is designed around a swappable ExecutionEnvironment interface (web-ui \| api \| mobile \| iot). Mobile and IoT are future execution targets beyond the immediate Web-UI + API scope of TD-013. Mobile: Appium/WebdriverIO or Playwright mobile emulation for native/hybrid apps — same Goal model, different executor (gesture actions, native element trees, device state vs DOM). IoT: device state observation + command execution (MQTT, REST, device APIs) — same Goal model, different executor (sensor readings, device registry, command acknowledgment). The Goal → Required State → Observed State → Action → Evidence → Next Goal loop is environment-agnostic by design; only the executor changes. | Low | DEFERRED — do NOT start until (a) TD-013 Web-UI + API execution environments are proven, (b) FORGE has real commercial demand for mobile/IoT testing, and (c) the ExecutionEnvironment interface is stable (finalized in TD-013 design). At that point, Mobile is Phase 1 (closer to Web-UI), IoT is Phase 2 (more novel). Log now to preserve the vision; build when the market and the architecture are both ready. |
| TD-101 | Agent DecisionLog — the planner should own an explicit log of every decision made during a crawl: {goal, optionsConsidered, selectedOption, reason, outcome}. Not needed for Phase 1 implementation, but becomes invaluable for debugging ("why did the planner choose Path B?"), learning, explainability, and future RL-style optimization (TD-086 OMLIP). Nova rated this the one missing piece from a 10/10 architecture. | Low · NB | Deferred — do NOT start until TD-013 Phase 1 agentic crawl is proven working. At that point, DecisionLog becomes a first-class architectural concept alongside Goals and Evidence. It also feeds naturally into the /evals framework (TD-085) as an eval input for the learning loop. |
| TD-102 | WebUIEnvironment.act() uses raw page.locator() — agent clicks/fills do not self-heal. The forgeExpect/SmartLocator healing path (TD-065) is a pass-through for raw locators. Real action-healing requires constructing a SmartLocatorDef from action.target and routing through SmartLocator.resolve() with AssertionContext. | Low | Wire SmartLocator-backed act() so agent click/fill heals when a selector breaks — same pattern as generated POM actions (TD-065 Commit 3). Do after WebUIEnvironment Phase 1 is proven. |
| TD-102b | ExecutionEnvironment interface missing init()/close() lifecycle methods. WebUIEnvironment and ApiEnvironment both implement init() (launch browser/context) and close() (dispose), but these aren't on the ExecutionEnvironment interface — AgentRunner uses the concrete union (WebUIEnvironment \| ApiEnvironment) to call them, bypassing the abstraction. Fix: add optional init?(): Promise<void> and close?(): Promise<void> to the ExecutionEnvironment interface in agent/types.ts. The MockEnvironment in verify-td013.test.ts would need stub implementations (no-ops). | Low · NB | Small types.ts + test change. Do when next touching agent/types.ts or when adding a third ExecutionEnvironment (mobile/IoT — TD-100). |
| TD-103 | AgentMemory persistence: migrate from JSON sidecar (models/<app>/agent-memory.json) to SQLite via Kysely. Phase 1 uses JsonAgentMemoryRepository (simple read/write, no migration needed). At scale, AgentMemory grows large across runs (evidence accumulates, goals multiply, confidence decays) and real queries become valuable ("show stale goals," "confidence decay trend," "UNREACHABLE history"). The existing SQLite DB + Kysely pattern (triage, healing, run history) is the right home. The AgentMemoryRepository interface (load/save/findStaleGoals) ensures the planner and AgentRunner never touch persistence directly — swapping JsonAgentMemoryRepository for SqliteAgentMemoryRepository requires zero planner changes. | Low · NB | DEFERRED — implement after (a) TD-013 Phase 2 is proven with JSON and (b) real scale demand exists (multiple apps, 100+ crawl runs, query needs). Migration pattern: new Kysely migration + new SqliteAgentMemoryRepository implementing the same interface. |
| TD-104 | `scripts/` directory is outside `check:core`'s tsconfig coverage (`include: ["src/**/*"]`) — `@ts-expect-error` probes in test files are unenforced (false-proof risk). Negative type proofs (e.g. `agentSupervised: false` rejected, `origin: 'invented'` rejected) currently require manual scratch compiles. Fix: add `scripts/` to a tsconfig so type-level negatives are permanently gated. | Low · NB | Measurement-integrity item — same family as the eval-harness cross-app id collision (TD-080). Surfaced during TD-093 Phase 2 Step 7. |
| TD-105 | `Mission.depthBudget` is stored in `AgentPlanner` (TD-093 Phase 2 Step 5) but not yet enforced — the planner does not cap exploration at `mission.depthBudget` hops. Enforcement deferred to TD-013 Phase 3 (goal auto-discovery design). | Medium · NB | Without enforcement, bootstrap `depthBudget=2` is documentation only, not a behavioral constraint. (In Phase 2, synthesized goals are depth-≤1 by construction, so the gap is currently latent.) **Enforcement intentionally deferred** — runtime goal expansion does not exist yet; `depthBudget` becomes active at the Phase-3 goal-spawn loop (Block 3). Not forgotten — dependency-gated. |
| TD-106 | `AgentRunner` writes `models/<app>/agent-memory.json` during bootstrap runs. The path is gitignored and consistent with existing AgentRunner behavior, but `bootstrap-manifest-<app>.json` does not record `agentMemoryPath` — the memory artifact is not traceable from the manifest. Minor polish: add `agentMemoryPath` to `BootstrapManifest` and populate in `runAgentPhase()`. | Low · NB | Traceability gap only — no correctness issue. Surfaced during TD-093 Phase 2 Step 6. |

## Platform UI (TD-UI series)

The `forge-ui/` foundation shipped (package, design system, API.md contract,
cloud-readiness stubs, Express server with 501 route stubs, React shell, `forge
ui` CLI). Each tab is built against the frozen API.md contract; every engine
call goes through `ExecutionContext`; engine (`src/`) never imports `forge-ui/`.

| ID | Description | Priority | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-UI-004 | Run tab — execute tests, SSE live results; run lifecycle RUNNING→COMPLETED/INTERRUPTED; per-test status in real time. Backend: ForgeStreamingReporter + DB-poll SSE. API: `POST /api/v1/runs`, `GET /api/v1/runs/:id/stream`. | High | **The SSE events (`run:started`/`test:completed`/`run:completed`/`run:interrupted`) are DEFINED in API.md but not emitted by TD-126 today** — their source (DB-poll of `test_results` by run_id) is this ticket. Corrects the brief's "locked in TD-126" premise. |
| TD-UI-005 | Results tab — AI triage verdicts, confidence, next steps; per-run view; HealStore records. Backend: AiTriageRepository + heal records. API: `GET /api/v1/results/:appName/:runId`. | High | |

Backend: TrendRepository (`getPassRateTrend`) + FlakyAnalysisRepository (`findByApp`). API: `GET /api/v1/insights/:appName`. | High | Insufficient-evidence rows render purple, never "0 flaky" (TD-120 honesty). |
| TD-UI-007 | Settings tab — AI budget slider, crawl depth, agent mode toggle (Supervised/Autonomous), credentials management. Backend: AppConfig read/write via workspace. API: `GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/:appName`. | Medium | |

Fallback: env var if `keytar` is unavailable. Deferred until Onboard-tab polish is complete. | Medium · NB | Currently the Onboard form passes credentials in-process to the crawl (never persisted), matching the engine's existing envKey-pointer model. |

Applying to existing generated files requires a regen. |
| TD-UI-015 | forge-ui production build not gated in CI. Add an `npm run build --workspace forge-ui` step to the Test job so UI build failures are caught in CI. | Low | Flagged 4×. Candidate for the next CI-hygiene pass alongside TD-125. |

| TD-UI-017 | Extend CredentialReference with a token/API-key variant — the env-pair model doesn't cover token auth (e.g. real Restful Booker). | Low · NB | Do NOT hack the resolver — it stays env-pair; add a typed variant. Surfaced confirming Restful Booker in Phase 6. |
| TD-UI-018 | **In-app contextual help (tab captions + per-control tips).** Shared `<HelpTip>` component (info icon + tooltip card, design-token styled, hover + focus, a11y) driven by a per-tab help-content map. Tier 1 — a tab caption per tab (Onboard / Crawl / Tests / Run / …). Tier 2 — per-control tips. Banked Crawl copy: Start Crawl / Force re-crawl / AI Budget (see Aiden). | Medium · NB | Build gated behind a Nova relay; NOT a Crawl-green blocker. Do not build yet. |

TD-UI-023 is the umbrella UX-design item; fold the chosen mechanism back into TD-UI-009 rather than duplicating. Design-gated + Nova relay before build. |
| TD-UI-024 | Collapse the ExecutionContext→JobRunner double error-wrap ('Error: Error: ...') into a single operator-facing surface. `ExecutionContext.runGuarded` does `error: String(err)` (adds one `Error:` prefix), then `JobRunner` does `new Error(result.error)` (adds another) — non-operator failures surface as `"Error: Error: …"`. Touches `runGuarded`'s error return and JobRunner's re-raise. Needs its own design pass; do not fix incidentally. | Low · NB | Surfaced during TD-UI-003 Block 4b. **Scope note:** the operator-facing path (OperatorFacingError → `errorCode`) already avoids the double-wrap (ExecutionContext preserves the raw `.message`, not `String(err)`), so this remaining defect affects only NON-operator-facing engine failures. |
| TD-UI-025 | Flaky real-HTTP UI tests. `verify-ui-authenticate.test.ts` (A2) and potentially `verify-ui-crawl` intermittently fail with `TypeError: fetch failed` under parallel `node --test` load — the test spins a real Express server via `app.listen(0)` and fetches it; the `fetch` can fire before the listener is accepting. Observed 2026-07-12 during TD-UI-003 Block 5a: run 1 = 290/1 fail, identical re-run = 291/0. Not a logic failure — a transport/timing race in the test harness. Root cause is test-infra fragility, not product code. Fix shape (needs design, do not patch blindly): await a real readiness signal from `listen()` before fetching, rather than relying on timing. | **High** (raised from initial Medium) | **Escalated after the CI check (TD-UI-003 Block 5a follow-up):** CI runs the IDENTICAL harness — `npm run test:unit` → `tsx --test scripts/*.test.ts` (`e2e-pipeline.yml:109`), same default `node --test` file-parallelism, **no `--test-concurrency` limit and no retry** on the step. So this flake can fail CI exactly as locally and turn the pipeline red on a transport race (false negative). FORGE's thesis is truthful test-suite health — a known intermittent failure in our own suite is not shippable. |
| TD-UI-026 | Crawl tab: crawl-state awareness, empty-state design, and evidence freshness. Currently the Crawl tab shows a bare project list until selection, and an already-crawled project renders an empty "ready" state with no indication a crawl ever happened. FORGE tells the user nothing about what it already knows. **Required states (Raj's design):** (a) NO APPS ONBOARDED → designed empty state: faded FORGE mark, "Onboard an application to begin", CTA to Onboard. (Today it reads as broken, not empty.) (b) ONBOARDED, NEVER CRAWLED → "Onboarded {timestamp}. FORGE hasn't explored this application yet." Button reads [Start Crawl]. (c) CRAWLED → last-crawl summary (page count, strategy, module breakdown) + "Last crawled {relative}" prominent, absolute timestamp beneath/on hover. Button reads [Start Re-Crawl]. The Start Crawl / Start Re-Crawl label distinction is a free honesty signal — the button itself tells the operator whether FORGE has ever seen this app. **RATIONALE (Raj, and this is the core of it):** an SDET landing on this tab must be able to answer "is my model stale?" before deciding whether to re-crawl. With no timestamp and no summary they either re-crawl blindly or — worse — generate tests off a stale model without knowing it. Relative time ("3 weeks ago") is decision-support; an absolute date makes them do the math. This is an evidence-freshness gap adjacent to TD-067 (FORGE must not be blind to the staleness of its own inputs), not a cosmetic one. **MECHANISM:** `JobRunner.currentExecution` clears on completion by design, so `GET /crawl/active` 404s and a remount shows ready-state. Fix shape: on mount with `?project=` and no active job, read the last persisted crawl result + timestamp from the workspace and render it — the same rehydration pattern TD-UI-003 uses for `GET /tests/manifest`. | High | Deferred behind Tests-tab Green (tab-by-tab rule). Warrants a Nova + Finn design pass on empty-state and staleness presentation before implementation. Related: TD-UI-005 (Results tab = primary home for historical viewing), TD-UI-022 (in-flight resume, which exposed this), TD-067 (input freshness). |
| TD-UI-032 | **GenerationManifest inherits the pattern.** `GeneratorRunner.ts:315-340` — a PARTIALLY empty model (pages > 0, flows = 0) stamps `generatedAt` / `durationMs` / `classificationRunId` alongside `specCount 0` / `observedFlows 0`. The fully-empty case is guarded (`:152`); the partial case is not. We built this today (TD-UI-003 Block 5) and inherited the same contract violation while fixing the fully-empty one. | Medium | Governed by ADR-015. Lower severity — the counts are honestly derived from files actually written; the issue is provenance (`generatedAt`/`durationMs`) stamped over near-zero output. |
| TD-UI-033 | Discovery tab — input ingestion UI. Browse button (OS file picker) + adjacent type field. Supported input types: Requirements/BRD, Design mockups/Figma, API contracts, Test Cases (TestRail/Zephyr/Xray/Jira CSV/Excel), Bug History (Jira/ADO export), Existing automation code, Production analytics (GA4/Mixpanel CSV — page hits, user path sequences, funnel drop-offs, error rates by page). **DESIGN (Aiden, per "derive don't declare"):** FORGE AUTO-DETECTS the input type from the file's structure/content and displays what it detected. The type dropdown is an OVERRIDE for when detection is wrong — not a declaration the user must make. Same UI, one less thing to get wrong. Each input type maps to a different interpretation strategy, but ALL converge on ONE normalized intent representation, which is then reconciled against the crawl model. Only the crawl produces GROUNDING; every document produces INTENT. **CORE CONSTRAINT:** a document is a CLAIM about the application, never evidence about it. When a document and the crawl disagree, THE CRAWL WINS — and the disagreement is itself a reportable finding. FORGE must never invent a selector to make a document-derived flow reachable. **LIVE SOURCES (not just file uploads):** Jira MCP is a planned adapter — Jira holds test cases, bugs, and user stories in one live source, better than stale CSV exports. Do NOT build the connector before the Discovery pipeline exists; the adapter must be designed AROUND the normalized intent representation, not the other way around. The Discovery architecture must anticipate live sources (MCP, REST) alongside file uploads from day one, so a connector is a swap-in adapter rather than a redesign. Prove the pipeline with a dumb CSV first — it forces the reconciliation logic to be honest. | High | Raj 2026-07-13. Feeds the Discovery design cycle. Related: TD-UI-026, the greenfield-vs-brownfield entry points. |
| TD-UI-034 | Extend API verification coverage so the deliberately-skipped set shrinks. VerificationRunner verifies only GET, non-path-param endpoints; mutations (non-GET) and path-param endpoints (`{id}`) are skipped for safety (never executed). Per the Path D ruling (ADR-015), an all-mutations API is correctly INSUFFICIENT-EVIDENCE — the real fix is to VERIFY MORE, not score softer: (a) verify path-param endpoints with sample IDs; (b) schema/contract read-verification of mutations (endpoint exists / contract shape) WITHOUT executing them. Shrinks the skip set so a healthy API can earn a real score. | Medium | Surfaced during TD-UI-029 Block C (Path D). Do NOT soften the score to compensate for skips — that manufactures the phantom greens ADR-015 forbids. VerificationRunner now surfaces the skip count prominently (`endpointsSkipped`). |
| TD-UI-035 | Migrate HealStore's `-1` confidence sentinel to nullable under ADR-015. `HealStore.ts:33` `UNVERIFIED_HEAL_CONFIDENCE = -1` (written at `:143`) is the correct-INTENT pattern (refuses to default to a plausible number) but predates ADR-015. Nova: "If designing today under ADR-015 I'd make that nullable too — strong typing prevents accidental arithmetic." Migrate `heal_events.confidence` + the in-memory field to `number \| null`; a null cannot be accidentally summed/averaged the way `-1` silently can. | Low | Surfaced during TD-UI-029 Block C. Deliberately NOT retrofitted in the TD-UI-029 work (Nova ruling: log and move on). Governed by ADR-015. |
| TD-UI-036 | Verification `confidenceLevel` uses UPPERCASE tiers (`HIGH`/`MEDIUM`/`LOW`) while the rest of FORGE uses lowercase confidence vocabulary (`high`/`medium`/`low`/`insufficient-evidence`/`unknown` — flaky-predictor DB enum, triage taxonomy, module confidence). TD-UI-029's new `insufficient-evidence` value joined the canonical lowercase form, leaving the tier casing mixed within the verification union. Normalise the verification tiers to lowercase. | Low · NB | Surfaced during TD-UI-029 Block C. Pre-existing local inconsistency — do not fix incidentally; needs a coordinated change across the report JSON, `platform.js` display, and any readers. |
| TD-UI-038 | Verification tier thresholds (HIGH ≥0.85, MEDIUM ≥0.65) were set under the OLD scoring model, where absent components silently contributed their full weight. Under component-aware scoring (TD-UI-029) the score distribution has shifted — e.g. a web app with perfect elements but an entirely unmeasured flow component now scores 0.6 (was masked as 1.0). Review whether the bands still carve the right lines, **with EVIDENCE from real runs, not intuition**. Do NOT adjust a threshold to make a specific result read better — that is the same reflex that produced the 0.6 default. | Low | Surfaced during TD-UI-029 Block E (V3). Governed by ADR-015. |
| TD-UI-037 | Verification must not write into the shared `runs` (test-execution) table — separate VerificationRun / ExecutionRun records (Nova's long-term view). `VerificationRunner` saveReport inserts a `runs` row with `total_tests`/`passed`/`failed` derived from element+flow checks and `metadata.type='verification'`. Aggregators that do NOT filter that type — `knowledge-query.ts` (passRate, failures/flaky at `:240`/`:252`) and `TrendRepository.getPassRateTrend` — count verification rows in the TEST pass-rate/trend, polluting them (a verification is not a test run). TD-UI-029 Block D's minimum fix (status never 'passed' → 'inconclusive') stops the phantom green, but the rows still contribute `total_tests`/`passed` to those aggregates. Proper fix: a distinct VerificationRun record, or an aggregator-wide `type` filter. | Medium | Surfaced during TD-UI-029 Block D. Governed by ADR-015 (a verification's provenance must never read as a test outcome). |
| TD-UI-039 | Gap remedies: every ungrounded flow, unverifiable page, and unclassified element must carry a structured, MACHINE-READABLE remedy — what specific input would close this gap. Per ADR-016. This is NOT a UI string; it is a field on the evidence model, so the UI, the CLI, and every future consumer surface the same targeted ask. Requires: a remedy taxonomy, and each gap-producing subsystem (FlowDetector, VerificationRunner, CrawlRunner, module classifier) emitting one. Must honour the remedy hierarchy — FORGE proposes self-closure (tier 1) before any operator ask (tier 2/3). | High | Raj 2026-07-13. The differentiating capability: "honesty earned, plus the exact information required to close the gap." Governed by ADR-016 (Map the Gap, Prescribe the Remedy). Related: TD-UI-033 (Discovery surface), TD-013 (agentic crawl as tier-1 remedy), TD-UI-034 (first tier-1 instance). |
| TD-UI-040 | **Crawl diagnostic capture — FORGE observes crawl failures, then discards the evidence.** Comprehensive retrofit populating `crawlMetadata.crawlDiagnostics` (the seam TD-UI-031 defines) from sites #3–#10: AuthManager, PageVisitor, ObservationSettlingPolicy, SelfCorrectionEngine, SPAStrategy. Each threads a machine-readable `reason` + `detail` + point-of-observation `remedy` out of a `catch`/degraded path that currently swallows it. **The full 10-site inventory table is the TD body — see §TD-UI-040 below (do not summarise it away).** | High | Raj 2026-07-14. Governed by ADR-015 (record the diagnostic, never erase it) + ADR-016 (crawled-empty must carry a remedy). Seam owned by TD-UI-031 (defines `crawlDiagnostics` + wires site #1). Related: TD-UI-039, TD-013 (agentic exploration as a tier-1 remedy). Part of the "observe-then-discard" systemic pattern (TD-UI-041). |
| TD-UI-041 | **SYSTEMIC AUDIT: FORGE observes evidence and discards it.** Three confirmed instances in two days: **(i) Navigation edges** — the crawler traverses A→B at crawl time but does not persist the edge; FlowDetector must then INFER flows from page names (`source:'agent-proposed'`, `confidence:'partial'`), and the generated code admits it (`// FORGE: navigation not observed during crawl (no real edge)`). The traversal happened; the evidence was thrown away. Likely root of FC-002 / FC-004a. **(ii) Page-load failures** — `Crawler.ts:187-193` bare `catch{}` swallows a start-URL `goto` failure entirely (TD-UI-040 site #2). **(iii) Login-wall diagnosis** — `StrategyDetector.ts:152` COMPUTES `realLinks===0 && jsClickables===0`, LOGS "Sparse page (login page etc)", then discards it (`detect()` returns only `mode`). FORGE diagnoses the most common empty-crawl cause and tells nobody. **(iv) Auth-required-but-no-credentials — the worst instance.** `ConfigAdapter` LOGS `"authType 'form-login' but no credentials — the crawl will run UNAUTHENTICATED"`, produces `roles: []`, and `Crawler.crawl`'s role loop then iterates ZERO times — the start page is never visited. FORGE explicitly warns the crawl cannot work, runs it anyway, and reports completion with 0 pages. It knows the crawl is useless, says so out loud, and proceeds regardless (surfaced during TD-UI-031 Block 4, saucedemo). Wired in TD-UI-031 Block 4 as `crawlDiagnostics` reason `'auth-required'` (distinct from `'auth-failed'`: never-tried vs tried-and-rejected). This is not three bugs — it is a HABIT, and it is why `crawled-empty` cannot carry a remedy (ADR-016) and why flows are inferred rather than observed (ADR-011). **AUDITED 2026-07-14** (read-only 6-cluster sweep of the whole engine): ~40 sites across crawl / classify / enrich / verify / heal / triage / flaky-scoring / notify / agentic. The full inventory — four ARCHETYPES + LIE / MUTE / MISSED tiers + the navigation-edge trace — is the TD body: **see §TD-UI-041 below (do not summarise it away).** Three findings severe enough for their own IDs are split out: **TD-UI-042 / 043 / 044.** | High | Raj/Aiden 2026-07-14. Governed by the proposed **ADR-017 ("What FORGE Observes, FORGE Keeps")** — upstream of ADR-015/016. Related: TD-UI-040 (crawl diagnostics — a subset), ADR-011 (Verify Before Assert). |

### TD-UI-040 — swallowed-diagnostic inventory (the TD body)

Every place the crawl path observes a failure/degradation and does not record it on the model. Ranked by remedy value. "Logged?" = console only; "On model?" = survives into the persisted artifact. Sites #1–#2 are wired by TD-UI-031 (site #1 is already computed); **#3–#10 are TD-UI-040's scope.**

| # | Site (file:line) | What's swallowed | Logged? | On model? | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `StrategyDetector.ts:152` | Computes `realLinks===0 && jsClickables===0` and diagnoses **"Sparse page (login page etc)"**; `detect()` returns only `mode` (`:165`). Counts + diagnosis discarded. | Yes (`:158`) | **No** | TD-UI-031 |
| 2 | `Crawler.ts:187-193` | Start-URL `goto` in `try/catch` → **bare `catch {}`** sets `crawlMode='bfs'`. A start page that never loaded. | **No** (bare catch) | No | TD-UI-031 |
| 3 | `AuthManager.ts:140-143` | `catch(e)` on the whole auth flow → `authenticated:false`. `e.message` names the failing step (goto / username-not-visible / submit). | Yes (`:141`) | Only `authOutcome:'failed'` — reason lost | TD-UI-040 |
| 4 | `AuthManager.ts:118-124` | `stillOnLogin` → `authenticated:false`. A specific diagnosis (credentials rejected / form didn't submit). | Yes (`:119`) | Only `authOutcome:'failed'` | TD-UI-040 |
| 5 | `AuthManager.ts:104` | `waitForURL` timeout → `catch {}` (comment "check if we moved"). | No | No | TD-UI-040 |
| 6 | `PageVisitor.ts:173-183` | Per-page `goto` failure → **degraded record** (`elements:[]`, `domHash:''`). Reason not on record; only `domHash===''` weakly distinguishes it from a genuinely empty page. | Yes (`:174`) | Weakly (`domHash:''`) | TD-UI-040 |
| 7 | `ObservationSettlingPolicy.ts:69-79, 94-98` | Hydration / `networkidle` timeout → "proceeding with available evidence." The SPA-never-rendered case. | Yes | No | TD-UI-040 |
| 8 | `SelfCorrectionEngine.ts:76-78` | Escalation (hybrid retry) threw → returns original pages. "We tried to recover and couldn't." | Yes (`:77`) | No | TD-UI-040 |
| 9 | `PageVisitor.ts:214, :155` | `extractLinks` failure → `[]`; `page.content()` failure → `''`. | No | No | TD-UI-040 |
| 10 | `SPAStrategy.ts:292, 295-302` | Navigation-recovery `goto` failures → empty `catch {}`. | No | No | TD-UI-040 |

**Shape (defined by TD-UI-031, filled by TD-UI-040):**
```
crawlMetadata.crawlDiagnostics: CrawlDiagnostic[] | null   // null = clean crawl
CrawlDiagnostic = {
  scope:  'start-page' | 'role' | 'page',
  target: string,                              // url or role id
  reason: 'page-load-failed' | 'auth-failed' | 'zero-clickables'
        | 'hydration-timeout' | 'navigation-error',   // MACHINE-READABLE enum, never free text
  detail: string,                              // e.message — human context
  remedy: { tier: 1 | 2 | 3, action: string }  // ADR-016 remedy, stamped at the point of observation
}
```

**Systemic note (Raj 2026-07-14):** this is the THIRD instance in two days of *FORGE observes something, then throws the evidence away* — (1) navigation edges discarded, (2) swallowed page-load failures, (3) `StrategyDetector.ts:152`'s discarded login-wall diagnosis. It may be a systemic habit worth its own audit (a candidate ADR: "record what you observed"). Flagged, not yet logged as a standalone item — awaiting Aiden's call.

### TD-UI-041 — observe-then-discard inventory (the TD body)

Read-only 6-cluster sweep, 2026-07-14 (whole engine, not just the crawler). ~40 sites. The four **archetypes** are the actual finding — they explain WHY this keeps happening and are what a structural fix (ADR-017) must address. The tiers below are symptoms.

#### The four archetypes (the finding)

1. **Boolean/enum type boundary eats the reason.** The WHY dies at the type, not the code: `AuthResult.authenticated` (a bare boolean, no `reason`/`failedStep`), `roleAuthOutcomes` 3-state, `isResolvable(): boolean`. Every diagnostic is destroyed before it can be stored.
2. **Lossy DB projection.** A DB table omits columns for what the stage computed — the file artifact has it, the system-of-record doesn't, the next consumer can't: `ai_triage` (no `evidence` column), `flaky_analysis` (`signal_*` written as 0).
3. **`console.*` as the only home** for a fact FORGE established. Pervasive — and sometimes *mandated* (ObservationSettlingPolicy's own docstring says "log and continue", which is exactly what turns it into a LIE at the consumer).
4. **Winners-only persistence.** Keep the survivor, drop the deliberation: rejected classifications, failed heals, blocked-goal reasoning, sub-threshold Vision calls.

#### LIE tier — discarding causes a false or unsubstantiated persisted assertion (ADR-015 violations, live in shipped code)

| # | file:line | Observed then discarded | The false/unsubstantiated assertion it produces |
|---|---|---|---|

| 5 | `AuthManager.ts:46-58` | auth **skipped** (unsupported flow / no creds) vs **failed** | Never-attempted auth collapses to the same `authenticated:false` as a rejected login |

#### MUTE tier appendix — FORGE knows something useful and cannot tell the next consumer (ADR-016 gaps)

- **Auth reasons:** `AuthManager.ts:140-144` (which step threw), `:118-124` (stillOnLogin), `:104-106` (waitForURL timeout, bare `catch{}`). Root: `AuthResult` is a boolean (`:20-24`) — no slot for WHY.
- **Crawl recovery/degradation:** `SelfCorrectionEngine.ts:76-79` (escalation crashed), `SPAStrategy.ts:289-306` (nav-recovery failed / partial edges), `Crawler.ts:198-199` (start-page bare `catch{}` → silent `bfs`), `PageVisitor.ts:173-183` (page-load fail reason — the `CrawlDiagnostic` seam at `types.ts:316-329` exists but isn't produced here).
- **Heal:** `SmartLocator.ts:119-124` (failed-heal never recorded — HealStore holds only successes; `'failed'` tier is dead code), `:180-187` (`isResolvable` destroys why-original-missed into a bool), `VisionHealer.ts:83-93` (declined-heal reasoning/confidence), `HealStore.ts:106-109` (corrupt-store → silent `{}`), `:130-153` (`catch{}` swallows all insert errors as "duplicate").
- **Classify/enrich (all bypass the `CrawlDiagnostic` channel for `console.warn`):** `AiResidueStage.ts:43-50` (budget-starved `unknown` ≡ genuinely-ambiguous `unknown`), `:71-77` (AI classify error), `ModuleClassifier.ts:140-145` (malformed-JSON), `FlowDetector.ts:311-320` (AI-proposed flows lost to truncation — richest diagnostic, fully dropped), `:232-235` (budget "heuristics only"), `:279-285` (AI threw), `:299-309` (rejected hallucinated-role candidates), `ElementClassifier.ts:541-566` / `:45-53` (AI-naming fallback vs never-needed conflated).
- **Verify/flow:** `VerificationRunner.ts:437-441` (flow auth mismatch → mis-reported as step failure).
- **Post-execution:** `ai-triage.ts:258-260` (`triage_model`/`tokens_used`/`similar_failures` zeroed — columns exist → **TD-UI-043**), `:262` (insert `catch{}`), `flakyScoring.ts:76-87` + `FlakyPredictorStage.ts:150-156` (`signal_*` written as 0 → **TD-UI-044**), `notifier.ts:442-455` (CRITICAL send-failure → console only), `ForgeStreamingReporter.ts:249-253` (ingestion batch failure → console; reconciler backfills rows, never the cause).

#### MISSED tier appendix — real opportunity, no dishonesty

`StrategyDetector` mode-rationale on a *successful* crawl (`:158-165`); `SelfCorrectionEngine.ts:52-58` ("N pages is the maximum discoverable" — a positive coverage signal); `ElementClassifier.ts:443-507` (which `critical` rule fired), `:241-251` (dedup suspicion); `FlowDetector.ts:229` (silent skip-when-≥3-candidates); `ai-triage.ts:180-181` (parse-error detail); `AgentRunner.ts:54-57` (autonomous→supervised downgrade reason); `SPAStrategy.ts:141/255/342` (sweep-only coarse trigger); `VerificationRunner.ts:521-577, 1098-1100` (original-strategy-failure reason, nearest-match probe error, DB-write-failed). *(`ForgeStreamingReporter.ts:205-213` `timedout`→`interrupted` — **RE-GRADED to MUTE (A1)** 2026-07-15: a globally-timed-out run is genuinely cut off, so `interrupted` + `completed_at:NULL` is honest; the defect is the enum collapsing `timedout`/`interrupted` — distinction unrecoverable — plus the skipped flaky analysis. NOT a LIE. See §Baseline #1, Ingestion.)*

#### Navigation-edge trace (CORRECTED against data, then FIXED)

**Correction (2026-07-14, grep-verified — Aiden's ruling).** The "highest-value single repair in the codebase" framing was WRONG. `grep '"elementId": "navigation"'` returns **0** across all 4 on-disk models AND both DBs' `model_json` blobs. The BFS fake-id and the hybrid proximity-edge are **real latent LIEs in code that produce ZERO fabricated ids today** — every reference app crawls SPA/API/agent, none of which exercise the BFS-anchor-nav → inferred-nav path. The 42 `// FORGE: navigation not observed` comments in the generated specs are the **already-honest** `elementId: null` + `grounding:'inferred'` fallback, not the lie. Also corrected: the hybrid case does NOT "overlap TD-037" (TD-037 is a different hybrid *discovery-skip* gap); and **BFS `goto`s the href, it never clicks the anchor** (so a joined edge is href-derived, not click-observed — the edge is still observed evidence).

The `(fromPage, clickedElement, toPage)` triple across the three paths:
- **SPA — kept (was already honest).** Real click → `matchClickedElement` → `ElementDefinition.id` (`SPAStrategy.ts:162` → `Crawler.buildRoleStateEdges:360-366` → `FlowDetector:166`); miss-fallback is the selector string, never a fake id.
- **BFS — FIXED (was a latent fake-id).** Built a real `<a href>` edge then hard-stamped `trigger:'navigation'` → `FlowDetector:166` `elementId:'navigation'`. Now the **JOIN**: `element.href ↔ edge.toUrl` (both resolved-absolute, from the same `a[href]` set) → the real clicked-element id, or **null** on miss (never `'navigation'`; `VerificationRunner:658` would have used it as a bogus selector). A joined edge is `grounding:'observed'`.
- **Hybrid / spa-without-edges — FIXED (was fabrication).** The edge itself was visit-order proximity (`discoveredArr[i]→[i+1]`), no traversal, no href — a navigation FORGE never made. Now emits **NO edge** (honest absence).

**Defensive fix** (`StateEdge.trigger` → `string | null` — ADR-017 archetype 1; BFS join-or-null; hybrid emits no edge; grounding derived from the join): stops the lie appearing the first time an MPA is crawled in BFS mode. `FlowStep.elementId` was already `string | null`; `grounding:'observed'|'inferred'` already existed (FC-002); only two consumers (SpecGenerator, VerificationRunner), both null-safe. Proven by `verify-td-ui-041-nav-edge` J1-J5. Upstream of ADR-011.

### Baseline #1 — full truth audit (structured index)

Read-only audit of all 13 subsystems (2026-07-14), against ADR-015/016/017. **The durable full record — every finding with observed / where-it-dies / remedy / code at file:line — is `notes/truth-audit-baseline-1.md`.** This section is the STRUCTURED, actionable view; it does not duplicate the per-finding evidence. Future nightly audits diff against the note.

**Totals.** ~**58 NEW** findings (**~11 LIE, ~22 MUTE, ~25 MISSED** — ForgeStreamingReporter `timedout` re-graded LIE→MUTE 2026-07-15) + ~**30 already-logged re-confirmed live** + **1 HIGH security defect (TD-UI-051)**. The **four cross-cutting patterns (TD-UI-047–050) are the spine** — as ADR-017's archetypes are for TD-UI-041, the patterns are the actual finding; the 58 individual findings instance them or group by subsystem.

**By ADR-017 archetype (NEW findings — which habit dominates).** Archetype **3** (console/log is the only home) ~**12**, the most common; archetype **1** (return type / enum eats the reason) ~**10**, close second; archetype **4** (winners-only / deliberation dropped) ~**8**; archetype **2** (lossy DB/presentation projection) ~**4**. The habit lives at the type and logging boundaries, not in one module.

**By subsystem (worst first; severity-weight = LIE×3 + MUTE×2 + MISSED×1).** Full per-finding lists are under each subsystem heading in the note.

| Subsystem | NEW | weight | Sharpest NEW LIE(s) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Crawl** | 11 | ~21 | 4 LIEs writing affirmatively-clean values straight into the App Model (the source of truth): `pagesSkipped:0` always (claims complete coverage never verified), stubbed `diff[]` (`flowsAdded:[]` = "diffed, none" when never diffed), flow-blind `aiBudgetStatus`, `crawled_by ?? 'human'` on a no-crawl stub. **✅ ALL 4 LIEs (A `pagesSkipped`, B `diff[]`, C `aiBudgetStatus`, D `crawled_by`/`generatedBy`) FIXED** — Crawl-LIEs milestone: A/B → null (not 0 / not []); C → both-pool weakest-truth (ADR-018); D → 'engine' + stub null (migration 015, no backfill). Real crawls now assert only what they know. Tests C1–C12. **✅ RESOLVED `81f4ec2`** (CI run 29443858193). CI witnessed C1–C12 + migration 015 applied + root tsc + test:unit (432/0); forge-ui tsc local-only per TD-UI-052. |
| **Verify** | 8 | ~17 | the most severe individual LIEs — `status<500` phantom-pass, no-op steps counted passed, vacuous assert-nav → phantom-green flows/endpoints that **inflate `confidenceScore`**. **✅ LIE-1 (`status<500`) + LIE-2 (no-op steps / vacuous assert-nav) RESOLVED `4a3573c`** (CI run 29436813523) — phantom-green milestone: `classifyEndpointResult` routes non-2xx to could-not-verify; `executeStep`→`StepOutcome` + `finalizeFlowStatus`; `assessReadiness` mirrors the flow axis (excluded from `failedFlows`, blocks `modelReady`, labeled distinctly); score math unchanged. Tests V1-V13, CI-witnessed (root tsc + test:unit 419/0); forge-ui tsc local-only per TD-UI-052. |
| **Agentic** | 9 | ~15 | **P4** — honesty types with no producers (`limitations:[]`, dead `CapabilityRegistry`, dangling `preconditionEvidenceIds`). **✅ P4 RESOLVED `fd569ff`** (CI run 29453793099). Declared-channel-no-producer (ADR-017 archetype 5): P4-A `environment-error` producer built (3 types reserved → TD-UI-055); P4-B `CapabilityRegistry` + `discoveredCapabilities` quarantined `@unimplemented` (no empty claim persisted); P4-C action-evidence persisted, scoped evidence-integrity invariant (legacy grandfathered → TD-UI-056). Tests A1-A4/B1-B2/C1-C5. CI witnessed the suite + throw-silent-on-fresh-clone; forge-ui tsc local-only per TD-UI-052. |
| **Ingestion** | 6 | **11** | **P1** `status:'passed'` on a no-run reaches the **headline pass-rate**; `timedout→interrupted` (**re-graded MUTE (A1)** — enum-collapse, distinction unrecoverable; NOT a LIE) |

**Ingestion weight recompute (timedout re-grade LIE→MUTE).** 6 NEW = 2 LIE (`results-store:364`, `ForgeStreamingReporter:209`) + 1 MUTE (`ForgeStreamingReporter:205-213` timedout) + 3 MISSED (`normalizeStatus`, `error_msg` first-only, fabricated `started_at`/`worker_index`). Weight = 2×3 + 1×2 + 3×1 = **6 + 2 + 3 = 11**. The re-grade is **−1** vs the erroneous all-LIE-timedout count (would have been 3×3 + 3×1 = 12); the note's earlier loose "≈14" is corrected to the computed 11. Order unchanged (Crawl 21 > Verify 17 > Agentic 15 > Ingestion 11). **Remedy (ADR-016, on the note entry):** add a `'timedout'` member to `RunLifecycle` (or persist raw `FullResult.status` in a `status_raw` column) so the distinction survives; record why flaky scoring was skipped.

**Re-confirmed (~30) — pointers, NOT duplicated.** Every already-logged site cited in the note was verified against current line numbers and is **still live**; they map to **TD-UI-040** (10-site swallowed-diagnostic inventory) and **TD-UI-041** (~40-site 4-archetype body). The re-confirmation records that TD-UI-040/041 remain almost entirely unremediated — only the split-outs (042, 043) and the nav-edge fix have shipped. Do not re-mint these.

**Reference-clean (converge fixes on these).** `inputHealth.ts`, `ModuleClassifier.classify`, `TestFileResolver.ts`, `JobRunner.ts`, `deriveFlowConfidence`, `AppModelRepository.upsert` — all separate applicability / evidence / outcome into distinct nullable fields; plus HealStore's dirty-gate + `-1` sentinel (ADR-015). These are the pattern the P1–P4 fixes should adopt.

**Do-not-reflag (verified no regression).** TD-UI-029, TD-UI-031, TD-UI-042, TD-UI-043, and the nav-edge fabricated-elementId fix — all intact in current code (baseline re-verified `crawlMetadata`/`evidenceState`, `toTriageRow` evidence persistence, the BFS href-JOIN, hybrid honest-absence).

## Design decisions captured (not bugs, but constraints to respect)

These aren't open defects — they're explicit sequencing/architecture calls already
made, included here so they don't get silently violated by future work.

| Topic | Decision |
|---|---|
| Dashboard build order | Do not start Dashboard implementation while TD-014 (or any open verification/crawl correctness issue) remains unresolved. A drill-down dashboard built on unreliable underlying signal surfaces that unreliability as dashboard noise. Resolve foundation issues first. TD-013 resolved — no longer a blocker. |
| Dashboard data architecture | Dashboard must be a view layer on top of existing data (HealStore, flaky predictor scores, AI triage classifications, `run-history.json`/`trends.json`) — not a standalone reporting pipeline with its own data model. Avoids two sources of truth drifting apart. |
| TD-031 / OrangeHRM scope dependency | `Crawler.crawl()` always computes `stateGraph` for every app (`buildStateGraph()` runs unconditionally), but `detectFlows()`'s gating (`hasConfigFlows && isSpa`) skips `identifyCandidates()`/`enrichWithAi()` entirely for OrangeHRM today — meaning OrangeHRM cannot exercise the AI-inferred-flow path at all, regardless of how TD-031 is resolved. TD-031's fix should not be considered validated against OrangeHRM's actual production pipeline, and should not be claimed as "done" for app-agnostic use, until this gating is separately reviewed and revisited. (TD-031's own evidence bypassed this gating directly — calling `identifyCandidates()`/`enrichWithAi()` without going through `detectFlows()` — specifically because of this; see TD-031's Resolved entry.) |
| Verification confidence ≠ behavioral correctness (TD-033/TD-047, Option D) | `VerificationRunner`'s confidence scores indicate DOM-presence (or HTTP-status, for API apps) — not real-user-session behavior. Decided not to change `VerificationRunner`'s navigation pattern or check criteria (rejected for cost/complexity); instead redefined the contract and added a `verificationTier` field (`'dom-presence'` \| `'http-status'` \| `'interaction'`) to `ElementResult`/`FlowResult` so the limitation is visible in the report rather than implicit. Behavioral correctness (does this element actually work in a real user session) is the generated-spec CI run's job, not verification's. **Future features must not treat verification HIGH confidence as a predictor of generated-test pass/fail** — Dashboard's planned confidence-based gating, coverage scoring, or any similar feature must read `verificationTier` alongside `status`, not `status`/`confidenceLevel` alone. |

---

## Resolved (TD-173 — the rendering absence-floor emits `unknown`, never a false `static-rendered`)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-173 | **`detectRenderingModel` could never emit `'unknown'`, so a MIGRATED model was more honest than a FRESHLY CRAWLED one.** No framework marker → `'static-rendered'` at the floor — but absence of a marker is not evidence of static rendering (an unhydrated framework app reads identically; live-confirmed on SauceDemo, Raj 2026-07-21: `#root` absent on first look, present moments later). ESCALATED to HIGH — a MEASURED defect (an honestly-labelled wrong answer is still wrong), blocking Onboard GREEN, with TWO parts: the floor VALUE and WHEN the sample is taken. | **`7e2783f`** / `2fbe165` (2026-07-23) | **RESOLVED.** Both parts fixed. **(1) Floor → `'unknown'`:** `detectRenderingModel` returns `value:'unknown', confidence:'unknown', source:'default-fallback'` when no marker is observed — never a false `'static-rendered'` (TD-173 asymmetry / ADR-020 §2). **(2) Timing:** the sample is taken after a fixed 2s delay (`Bootstrap.ts` detectRenderingModel; the method label reads as a delayed SAMPLE, not verified hydration — Nova R1). **Vocabulary retired:** `'static-rendered'` removed from the schema enum (`models/schema/app-model.schema.json` → `["framework-rendered","unknown"]`) — it had no producer once the false floor was removed; `ModelMigrator` normalizes any stored `'static-rendered'` → `'unknown'` (a fresh crawl re-observes). Gate: `npm run check` clean · `test:unit` 539/539 · forge-ui clean. Tests: `verify-td163-rendering.test.ts` (R2 `unknown` floor, R3 no retired value, R5b migration). Related: ADR-020 §2, ADR-021 (a dated note records the retirement, 2026-07-23), TD-163 (the refactor that surfaced it), TD-110 (the unhydrated-framework blind spot). |

## Resolved (TD-140 — the vacuous-green generated spec is refused, and the refusal propagates to the run aggregate)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-140 | **Vacuous-green generated spec: a test whose every step is honestly omitted still PASSED.** A generated spec whose every step carried `FORGE[omissionReason=interaction-never-observed / prerequisite-unverified]` left a test body with no executable assertion — it passed vacuously, a green tick exercising nothing. The omissions were CORRECT (ADR-011 honest refusal); the defect was the OUTCOME: zero evidence must not read as pass. **Breadth measured 2026-07-18:** 54 generated tests / 8 spec files; **1 fully vacuous** (`inferred-flow-standardUser-1782937629141 :: TC-GEN-002`, 6 omissions, zero executable lines); 9 partial (honest partials, NOT vacuous). | **`185af42`** (2026-07-23) | **RESOLVED — generation-time enforcement + ADR-018 §3 aggregate propagation (Nova-approved: site=generation, detection=structural, reuse could-not-verify→'unknown', not 'partial').** **Generation** (`SpecGenerator.ts`): `emitStep` now returns a classified `EmittedStep` (`{lines, executable, omissionReason}`) — vacuity is tracked STRUCTURALLY as the body is built (`executableStatementCount === 0`), never by parsing rendered source (Nova R1). `renderTest` emits `test.skip` + a `forge:could-not-verify` annotation + the evidence-based refusal wording when zero executable statements remain; a test with ≥1 executable statement is emitted unchanged (byte-identical). **Aggregate** (`testResultExtraction.ts` `regradeStatus`, `ForgeStreamingReporter.ts`): the one shared re-grade law now routes an ANNOTATED skip → could-not-verify (an ordinary developer skip stays skipped), so `deriveRunOutcome` gives the run `'unknown'` — a run containing a vacuous refusal never reads as fully passed. The reporter routes through `regradeStatus`, removing its duplicated inline logic (one law). **Evidence** (`GenerationManifest.ts` schema v3, `GeneratorRunner.ts`): `vacuousTestCount`/`partialTestCount`/`omittedStepCount` + a `refusals[]` detail array; refusals logged, never silent. **Proof:** empirically confirmed the annotation survives on a skipped test's `result.annotations` (PW 1.58); regenerating saucedemo from the HEAD model changed exactly 1 of 6 specs — TC-GEN-002 vacuous-body → `test.skip` — the other 5 byte-identical. Test `verify-td140-vacuous-refusal.test.ts` V1–V6. Gate: `npm run check` clean · `test:unit` 545/545 · forge-ui check clean. Related: ADR-018 §2 ("a step that ran no assertion is not a passed step") + §3 (composition), ADR-015 (Corollary 2), TD-UI-030 (sibling — the reporter's zero-SPEC case, already guarded), TD-064 (FC-004a honest omission, the source of the omissions). |

## Resolved (TD-UI-030 — reporter zero-spec false-pass was already fixed; stale-ledger reconciliation)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-UI-030 | **ForgeStreamingReporter reported a zero-spec run as PASSED.** As originally filed: a run reaching `onEnd` with `total_tests: 0` defaulted its outcome to `'passed'` (a `failed > 0 ? ... : 'passed'` ternary at `ForgeStreamingReporter.ts:209`) with no "did anything actually run?" check — a suite that executed nothing reported green. | **`a0c57e2` (2026-07-16)**, verified by audit **2026-07-23** | **RESOLVED — already-fixed, ledger never reconciled (same stale-ledger class as TD-064).** **The guard:** the verdict is no longer a defaulting ternary — `ForgeStreamingReporter.ts:233` (and the authoritative batch writer `results-store.ts:374`) delegate to `deriveRunOutcome` (`src/core/pipeline/testResultExtraction.ts:93`), where `realExecuted === 0` routes to `'unknown'`, **never `'passed'`** (ADR-015 Corollary 2: an outcome field must never default to success; ADR-018:186: a no-run run is not a pass). Pinned by `scripts/verify-adr018-redside.test.ts:171` (`realExecuted:0 → 'unknown'`). **Why the row was stale:** it quoted the `failed > 0 ? ... : 'passed'` ternary at `ForgeStreamingReporter.ts:209` that no longer exists — the fix (`a0c57e2`, "ADR-018 red-side: uncertainty survives heal → status → run → trend") landed 2026-07-16, and the row was later touched 2026-07-19 (`54e0797`) for a dedupe/annotate chore **without** reconciling the description against the fix that already existed. **LIVE SUCCESSOR — TD-140 (stays OPEN):** the same principle (zero evidence must not read as pass) survives open at the GENERATOR layer, where a vacuous spec (every step honestly omitted per FC-004a) still *executes* and therefore *passes* — so `realExecuted ≥ 1` and this `realExecuted === 0` guard structurally cannot catch it (measured 2026-07-18: 1 fully vacuous spec of 54). Related: ADR-015 (Corollary 2), ADR-018 (weakest truth), TD-140 (sibling at the generator layer), TD-064 (the stale-ledger precedent). |

## Resolved (TD-162 WAD + TD-163 ADR-021 rendering refactor — bootstrap detection)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-162 | **StrategyDetector counts ZERO signals where signals demonstrably exist; the wrong strategy is then graded `evidence-matched`.** Found by Raj's manual Onboard verification at `b421a2d` (standalone CLI crawl), three live sites. **Decisive case — Wikipedia, SAME page, SAME run, two detectors contradict:** `appType` observed `links=376, forms=1 → desktop-web`, while `crawlStrategy` observed `realLinks=0, jsClickables=5, spa=false → 'spa'`, `medium`, `source: evidence-matched`. Wikipedia has 376 links; `realLinks=0` is not a borderline read, it is impossible — and off that zero the detector concludes `'spa'` for a classic multi-page site AND now asserts the wrong value as positively evidenced. **Corroborating (same session):** SauceDemo `crawlStrategy` reason `"SPA markers, links, and JS-clickables all absent"` on a page where `appType` simultaneously observed `spaDom=1`; OrangeHRM identical. **Direction rules out timing:** `crawlStrategy`'s stated method is an immediate DOM count at domcontentloaded PLUS a 2s wait — it observes strictly LATER than `appType`'s immediate count, yet reports FEWER signals. A longer window cannot legitimately find less than a shorter one → the fault is in the COUNTING (selector, frame targeting, or evaluation context), not hydration timing. **Consequence:** crawl mode is selected from a measurement that does not reflect the page (under-crawl / wrong-strategy crawl follows). | WAD 2026-07-21 — no code change (works as designed) | **NOT · NB — a live measurement error whose wrong value is now reported as evidence-matched at medium.** Per Nova's TD-156 ruling this is primarily a QUALITY defect; the ADR-020 grading is behaving CORRECTLY — it is faithfully grading a broken measurement (the honesty consequence of a quality defect is why this is High, not the grading itself). **Explicitly NOT a regression from TD-158/ADR-020** — TD-156 removed the lie about confidence, not the error in the observation (exactly the quality-vs-honesty split Nova ruled). **Investigation before fix — determine why StrategyDetector's counts disagree with DOM reality before proposing anything; do NOT patch on contact.** Found by Raj's manual Onboard verification, 2026-07-20, at `b421a2d`. Related: TD-156 (strategy quality vs honesty), TD-163 (sibling appType measurement defect — same session, opposite mechanism: this UNDER-counts, that OVER-matches), ADR-020 (the provenance strings that made this visible). **CLOSED 2026-07-21 — WORKS AS DESIGNED (Nova+Finn/Aiden/Raj). NOT a counting failure.** Direct Playwright evidence (`www.wikipedia.org`, detection's exact conditions): raw `a[href]` = 376 (both `locator` and `querySelectorAll` agree); `realLinks` filter breakdown = `{total:376, sameOriginDiffPath:0, crossOrigin:375, samePath:0, hashOrJs:1}`. `realLinks` is DEFINED as same-origin, different-pathname (`StrategyDetector.ts:108-121`); 375 of 376 anchors are cross-origin (en/de/…wikipedia.org — different origin than `www.wikipedia.org`) and 1 is a hash link, so **0 is accurate**. The original "376 vs 0 is impossible" framing **compared two DIFFERENT metrics** — `appType.links` (raw anchor count) vs `crawlStrategy.realLinks` (same-origin navigable count) — never the same measurement (the exact ADR-021 presentation defect). **This establishes FORGE as a SAME-ORIGIN APPLICATION crawler, not a general web crawler.** Cross-origin portal crawling is a FUTURE CAPABILITY DECISION, not a detector correction — **must NOT be smuggled in as a fix here.** Related: ADR-021 (the presentation rule; the metric rename `realLinks → sameOriginNavigableLinkCount`). |
| TD-163 | **appType SPA-marker fires on structurally OPPOSITE applications; `spaDom=1` does not discriminate.** Same verification session (`b421a2d`, live). SauceDemo (a classic MULTI-PAGE app) and OrangeHRM (a GENUINE SPA) produced IDENTICAL appType output: `value spa, spaDom=1, spaScript=0, medium, evidence-matched`. A marker that fires on both a multi-page app and a real SPA separates nothing — the observation does not discriminate the classes it is used to distinguish. Wikipedia, by contrast, fell to the nav-and-form branch and read `desktop-web` correctly, so the defect is in the **SPA-marker branch specifically** (`spaDom>0 → 'spa'`), not in `detectAppType` as a whole. The value shown for SauceDemo (`spa`) is WRONG, and it is wrong at `medium`/`evidence-matched`. | 0c38a31 / 0c81b4d / 845e513 (on origin) | **ADR-019 axis 2 (discriminative competence) at the MARKER level — NOT a grading problem (the grade is honest).** `spaDom=1` (a `#root`/`#app`/`[ng-version]`/`[data-reactroot]` count) is satisfied by structurally different applications, so `'spa'` is not uniquely warranted by it. Sibling of TD-162 in symptom (same session, both wrong at medium/evidence-matched) but DISTINCT in mechanism — TD-162 under-counts, this over-matches. **Investigation before fix — determine what `spaDom` actually matches and whether the marker set can discriminate at all before proposing a change; do NOT patch on contact.** Found by Raj's manual Onboard verification, 2026-07-20, at `b421a2d`. Related: ADR-019 (axis 2), TD-162 (sibling), TD-157 (appType single-pre-auth surfacing), ADR-020 (the grade is correct; the observation is not). **REWRITTEN 2026-07-21 — SEMANTIC CLAIM ALIGNMENT defect under ADR-021 (Nova+Finn/Aiden/Raj). The original framing above ('marker fires on structurally opposite apps' / ADR-019 axis 2) is SUPERSEDED.** Direct Playwright evidence: SauceDemo genuinely contains `<div id="root">` — the selector resolved and returned its `outerHTML` (the real login page inside `#root`); `locator`=1 and `querySelectorAll`=1 agree; main frame, correct URL, present at `domcontentloaded`, no timing artifact. **So this is NOT a counting defect (the count is accurate) and NOT ADR-019 axis 2 (the observation is unambiguous — `#root` IS present).** It is ADR-021: `detectAppType`'s marker set observes FRAMEWORK MOUNT-POINT PRESENCE (rendering) and its output field claims NAVIGATION ARCHITECTURE (`'spa'` = routing). SauceDemo is React-rendered AND full-page-reloads on login (a document request to `inventory.html`, confirmed by network-panel inspection). The count was right; the claim was wrong — **client-side RENDERING does not imply client-side ROUTING.** Remediation: the scoped refactor (emit what is observed — framework-rendered vs static-html — not what is inferred), governed by ADR-021, blast-radius mapped 2026-07-21. Related: ADR-021 (the governing law), TD-157 (appType single-pre-auth surfacing). |

|---|---|---|---|
| TD-UI-051 | **SECURITY (HIGH) - unvalidated `appName` -> filesystem path traversal in forge-ui.** Client-supplied `appName` (route params + POST bodies) was only type-checked, then flowed unsanitized into `path.join(os.homedir(), '.forge-projects', appName)` for `WorkspaceResolver.resolve()` (reads) + `provision()` (mkdirs). `appName='../../../x'` escaped `.forge-projects` (arbitrary-parent `readJson` via `GET /:appName/tests/manifest` + `GET /:appName`; mkdir outside the root). No path-segment validation existed anywhere in `forge-ui/server` (grep-confirmed). | `6c2aa35` (fix + `verify-td-ui-051-appname.test.ts` S1-S12/B1-B3/R1-R3/E1-E3, 23/0) | **CI-proven: run `29428407664`, headSha `6c2aa35`, success** - security suite executed in CI, `test:unit` 406/0, root tsc green. **Fixed** via shared validator `forge-ui/server/context/appName.ts` (charset `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$`); defense-in-depth - route-entry 400 (`router.param('appName')` + POST-body guards), `WorkspaceResolver.resolve/provision` throw `InvalidAppNameError` backstop, `TestFileResolver` RETHROWS (no-oracle null preserved for the file-absent axis, R2/R3). **HONEST GAP:** forge-ui tsc does NOT run in CI (the pipeline does not type-check `forge-ui/` - TD-UI-052); forge-ui type-safety verified LOCALLY only (`tsc --noEmit` exit 0) - CI proves runtime behavior via tsx transpile+execute, not a full forge-ui type-check. Baseline #1 (2026-07-15); NOT an honesty archetype - a standalone boundary defect mirroring the `TestFileResolver` threat model. Follow-ups: TD-UI-052 (CI forge-ui-typecheck gap), TD-UI-053 (dedicated remedy field). |

## Resolved (TD-UI-043 — the triage evidence gate no longer evaporates in the record)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-UI-043 | **The triage evidence-gate held at runtime and evaporated in persistence.** The AI's `evidence` — REQUIRED by the gate (`ai-triage.ts:454`, TD-063) which downgrades an app-bug that has reasoning but no evidence — was computed, gated on, carried on `TriageResult`, then DISCARDED on the DB write (`ai_triage` had no `evidence` column). So a persisted app-bug verdict was **unsubstantiated in the system of record** (evidence ≠ reasoning: `root_cause` kept the narrative, not the gate-required proof). `triage_model`/`tokens_used` were hardcoded `''`/`0` into existing columns because `TriageResult` had no field for them (ADR-017 archetype 1: the type ate the value). Fixed: migration 014 adds `ai_triage.evidence` (text, NULLABLE — NULL = "none required"); `withUsage()` records **the model that ANSWERED** (never `CONFIG.model`) + real tokens; `toTriageRow()` writes evidence (`\|\| null`). `similar_failures` left as-is (never computed → TD-UI-046). | `7e64a7c` (migration 014 + `ai-triage.ts` + types + `verify-td-ui-043-evidence` T1-T5) + `6de0088` (docs) | **CI-proven: run `29370190659`, headSha `6de0088`, success** — `[migration] ✓ 014_ai_triage_evidence` applied in CI; the evidence-gate executed server-side. **Note (evidence over claims):** the CI run was fully green (`✅ All tests passed — nothing to triage!`), so triage wrote 0 rows this run — the first *production* write of evidence/model/tokens will be the next run with a real failure. Live persistence is proven by **T3** (real DB round-trip through the production `toTriageRow` + migration 014: an app-bug row read back carries its gate-required proof). `test:unit` 378/378. Two consumers unblocked: `adaptive-fixes` (ground fixes in evidence), `knowledge-query`/"Ask" (answer "what's the proof?"). Governed by ADR-017 (archetypes 1+2) + ADR-015; relates to TD-063. Split from TD-UI-041 (LIE #8). |

## Resolved (TD-UI-042 — the notifier no longer lies about failures)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-UI-042 | **FORGE emailed/Slacked "No failures detected" / "• None" on a run that FAILED** — a flat, false statement delivered to a human, in production, about the one thing FORGE exists to report; a red alert (`🚨 CRITICAL`, `App Bugs: N`) contradicting itself two blocks down. Root: `notifier.ts:159` hardcoded `failures = []` with the comment "detailed RunFailure shape not stored in ai_triage" — **which was FALSE**: `test_id` is `${file}::${title}::${browser}` (parseable) and `failure_category` IS the verdict, so `RunFailure` was fully reconstructible from rows the notifier already held. Fixed DB-first (no schema change): reconstruct from `triageRows`; add the honest-floor gate `(failed>0 \|\| bugs>0) && failures.length===0` → CANNOT-ENUMERATE + remedy (ADR-016), never "no failures"; both renderers; `+N more` so `slice(0,5)` stops silently under-reporting. | `68d1d0f` (fix + `verify-td-ui-042-notifier` 7/7) + `b2421fb` (ADR-017 + audit body) | **CI-proven: run `29367608388`, headSha `b2421fb`, success** — the AI Pipeline job (which RUNS the fixed notifier) passed. `test:unit` 372/372; root + forge-ui `tsc` clean. **N3 is the anti-regression guard:** the literal strings "No failures detected" and "• None" are asserted ABSENT on a failing run in BOTH renderers — the build fails if the lie returns. **The finding (Aiden):** *"The `:159` comment was FALSE, written next to the parseable `test_id` + `failure_category` that make it reconstructible, in a file that already names the artifact holding the answer (`TRIAGE_PATH`), whose actual field (`results[]`) never even matched the interface (`failures[]`) that would have read it. Four layers of the same disconnect in one function — this path was never once executed against reality."* Governed by ADR-017 (archetype 2, lossy DB projection) + ADR-015. Split from TD-UI-041 (LIE #9). Follow-up: TD-UI-045 (dead `TRIAGE_PATH`/`HISTORY_PATH` constants). |

## Resolved (TD-UI-031 — Provenance at the source: crawlMetadata + evidenceState)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-UI-031 | **CrawlRunner was the origin of the provenance lie** — the model, crawl-summary, and DB row carried `crawledAt` + `classificationRunId` + `page_count 0` when the crawl found nothing, and the Generator/Verify `EmptyModelError` guards were downstream compensation for a producer that lied at the source. Fixed at the producer under ADR-015: crawl-execution provenance moved off `app` (identity) into a **nullable `app.crawlMetadata` container**, `evidenceState` (`crawled` / `crawled-empty` / `unsupported-platform`) authored **at the producer** from observed content, and a schema `if/then` invariant that makes the lie **unrepresentable** (`crawled` with null metadata, or `unsupported-platform` with metadata, are both REJECTED). Reaching for `crawlMetadata.crawledAt` on a never-crawled model is now a TYPE ERROR, not a plausible timestamp. Shipped in 5 blocks: (1) engine+schema cutover, (2) DB migration 013 (`crawled_at` nullable + `evidence_state`; NULL for unsupported-platform, never `''`), (3) shipped `forge migrate` (idempotent, backs up, refuses unmigratable models), (4) crawl diagnostics + corrected `EmptyModelError`, (5) TD-UI-041 doc. `'onboarded'` was DROPPED — it had no producer (a declared state with no evidence is the exact thing ADR-015 forbids). | Block 1 `414257b` (engine/schema) + `317a33d` (ADR-016 docs); Block 2 `7ac9c41` (DB); Block 3 `f7a3efb` (migrate); models `78c50e0`; Block 4 `f1d4b21` (diagnostics + error); docs `878572b` (TD-UI-041 iv) | **CI-proven:** Block 1 run `29343715317` (headSha `317a33d`) + Blocks 2–4 run `29357176898` (headSha `878572b`) — both **success** (Playwright suite + AI Pipeline). `test:unit` 365/365; root + forge-ui `tsc` clean. **Live saucedemo crawl (no creds) → `crawled-empty` with `crawlDiagnostics[0]` reason `'auth-required'` + tier-2 remedy**; corrected operator string states the crawl RAN and found nothing (the false "onboarded but never crawled" is gone; G8 guards its return). **Ruling 1 (recorded exactly):** Aiden asserted a 3/1 split from memory; CC's first migration reported 2/2 and refused to force it; the **cleanup (restore-HEAD-before-commit)** revealed the working tree had **saucedemo stripped to 0 pages** (HEAD: 5 pages/7 flows) → corrected **saucedemo = crawled**, **ultimateqa = the single genuine empty crawl**. The "two empty crawls" finding was itself a working-tree artifact. *"Twice in one block, committing dirty state would have produced a lie in the artifact — a mislabelled model, and a fabricated finding in the resolution note of the very TD whose purpose is eliminating false state. The cleanup was correctness, not hygiene." — Aiden.* Absorbed the original TD-UI-028 scope. Follow-ups open: **TD-UI-040** (crawl diagnostic capture, sites #3–#10), **TD-UI-041** (observe-then-discard systemic audit). |

## Resolved (TD-UI-029 — Verification Scoring: absence no longer reads as perfection)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-UI-029 | **VerificationRunner scored 1.0 / HIGH / "Model is ready" for a verification that verified ZERO elements and ZERO flows** — the CRITICAL honesty-floor bug. The `0.6`/`0.4` literals were WEIGHTS used as no-evidence DEFAULTS ("no elements verified" scored identically to "every element passed"), and a page that failed to load pushed no result so its elements vanished from the denominator. Fixed with component-aware scoring (ADR-015 — Applicability ≠ Evidence ≠ Outcome): applicable-but-absent keeps its weight in the denominator; not-applicable is excluded; no applicable evidence → `null` / `insufficient-evidence` (never a default). Plus could-not-verify results (a failed-to-load page counts, distinct from a model defect), an earned "Model is ready" gate (Nova's five conditions), and the DB row writes `'inconclusive'`, never `'passed'`. | `0af6f93` (engine) + `7ab1cb0` (V1-V7 tests) | **CI-proven: run `29288205556`, headSha `71b99c2`, conclusion success** (unit tests incl. the 20 new V-tests + type checks + Playwright Stable suite all green). **Live reference-app sweep (before → after):** saucedemo (empty model) `1.0/HIGH/ready` → `EmptyModelError`; restful-booker (api) `1.0/HIGH` with a phantom `0.4` flow default → `1.0/HIGH` honestly (flows N/A, renormalized) + 6 mutation skips surfaced; **orangehrm `≈1.0/HIGH/"Model is ready"` (35 unverifiable elements had VANISHED from the denominator) → `0.97/HIGH` but "Model is NOT ready" (35 could-not-verify counted in 844/881 + gate).** Governed by ADR-015. Follow-ups open: TD-UI-034 (API coverage), TD-UI-035 (HealStore −1→nullable), TD-UI-036 (tier casing), TD-UI-037 (verification out of the runs table), TD-UI-038 (threshold review). |

## Resolved (TD-126 — ForgeStreamingReporter / Result Ingestion Layer)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |

| TD-126 | `ForgeStreamingReporter`: Playwright Reporter API adapter for FORGE's Result Ingestion Layer. DB path priority: workspace auto-discovery → reporter option → `DB_PATH` env var (Nova Q1). Run lifecycle (Nova Q2): `onBegin` creates the run (`lifecycle:'running'`); `onTestEnd` streams rows via a 100ms write queue; `onEnd` updates lifecycle (`'completed'`/`'interrupted'`) + status (outcome) + runs AnalysisPipeline after persistence. `--list` guard: all three hooks early-return in list mode — zero phantom runs. ## Resolved (Session 7)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | `SPAStrategy.crawl()` never visited `startUrl` itself, and discovered URLs were marked visited during discovery (not just during the visit phase), causing Phase 3 to skip all of them — SauceDemo crawl reported 1-2 pages instead of 8. | This session, prior to commit `0c46e53` | Fixed by visiting `startUrl` first and separating a `candidates` set (used during discovery dedup) from `visited` (used to gate actual page visits). |
| — | `AiBudgetTracker.remaining` was captured by value at construction time and never updated — `consume()`/`isExhausted()` correctly saw the live value via closure, but `.remaining` itself always reported the initial limit, making budget-exhaustion logs misleading. | `48113e8` | Fixed with a `tracker` object + getter so `.remaining` reads live state. |
| — | `ElementClassifier.nameWithAi` sent all unnamed elements on a page in one AI call regardless of count, capped at `maxTokens: 500` — pages with >20-ish elements (e.g. OrangeHRM's `viewEmployeeList` at 77 elements) returned truncated/malformed JSON and silently fell back to generic names. | `48113e8` | Fixed by batching into chunks of 20 elements per AI call, `maxTokens` raised to 1024, with per-batch logging instead of a single silent failure. |
| — | `FlowDetector` emitted the same bare credential placeholder (`{{KEY}}`) for both username and password fill steps. `VerificationRunner.resolveValue` expected `_USERNAME`/`_PASSWORD` suffixes to split the `user:pass` env var — without them, both fields resolved to the raw colon-joined string, login failed silently, and every flow failed at the post-login `assert-navigation` step. | `0c46e53` | Fixed in two locations in `FlowDetector.ts` (the generic input mapper and the explicit username/password step builder) by stripping any existing `_CREDENTIALS` suffix from `credentialsEnvKey` and appending the correct suffix per field. |
| — | `SPAStrategy.discoverViaButtonText` could click "Add to cart" / "Remove" style buttons during exploration — these matched the nav-button text filter (`/cart/i`) but don't navigate, so clicking them only had the side effect of silently mutating app state (populating the cart) during a crawl. | `0c46e53` | Fixed by adding a `MUTATING_TEXT_PATTERNS` exclusion list checked before treating a button as a navigation candidate. Verified safe on both SauceDemo and OrangeHRM — the excluded buttons never produced URL changes anyway, so no legitimate discoveries were lost. |
| — | Self-healing in `VerificationRunner` would promote whichever strategy worked to primary with no confidence-tier check — a low-confidence `text` match could silently displace a reliable `data-test`/`id` strategy, risking model corruption from a loose text match landing on unrelated content (e.g. footer copy). | `0c46e53` | Fixed by adding an `isDowngrade` check: a `text`-type heal is never allowed to displace a `data-test` or `id` primary strategy; logged as a kept-primary event instead of silently promoting. |
| TD-013 | `VerificationRunner` navigated directly to a page's URL with no prerequisite state setup, breaking verification of stateful pages (`cart.html` on SauceDemo has no per-item content until an item is added). | this session | Added `PageDefinition.prerequisites` (reuses the existing `FlowStep`/`executeStep()` execution engine — no new interpreter) compiled from a new app-specific `OnboardingConfig.pagePrerequisites`, executed in `VerificationRunner` right before a page's direct navigation. Failures are recorded to a new, separate `VerificationReport.setupFailures` array — never folded into `elementResults` — and force `confidenceLevel` down from HIGH regardless of the element/flow ratio. Verified live against SauceDemo: happy path passed 103/103 elements incl. all 22 `cart-html` criticals with `setupFailures: []`; induced failure (bad elementId) correctly logged `⚠ Prerequisite step 1 (click) failed`, dropped `elementsTotal` to 81 (no conflated selector failures), populated `setupFailures`, and capped confidence at MEDIUM despite a 1.00 raw ratio. `FlowDetector.mergeConfigSeeded()`'s separate hint-truncation gap (no flow ever reaches `cart-html`) intentionally not bundled — logged as TD-016. |

## Resolved (Session 10 — TD-018 root-cause fix)

| ID | Description | Resolved in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-018 | `PomGenerator.generatePage()`/`generateSmartProp()`/`generatePlainProp()` never deduplicated elements by `name` within a page, and the underlying `id` (`{pageId}:{name}`, the SmartLocator/HealStore key) collided too — not just the generated property name. Root cause was upstream in `ElementClassifier`: `determineName()` derives names from DOM attributes shared across repeated structural elements (SauceDemo's 6 product cards all carry `data-test="inventory-item-name"` etc.), and `nameWithAi()`'s independent 20-element batches can't see each other's choices, so both paths can produce same-page collisions. | this session | Fixed at the root, not in `PomGenerator`'s output step. Added generic (no app-specific knowledge) repeated-container detection to `ElementClassifier.harvestElements()`'s `page.evaluate()`: walks an element's ancestor chain for the nearest node with 2+ siblings sharing its tag+class signature, recording `containerIndex` (position among siblings) and `containerHint` (first short text found inside that container instance) on `RawElement`. A new `deduplicateNames()` pass runs after AI naming and guarantees uniqueness via three tiers: (1) a `containerHint`-based descriptive suffix where the hint is itself unique within the collision group (`inventoryItemName` → `inventoryItemNameSauceLabsBackpack`, ...); (2) a positional `containerIndex` suffix for members where the hint isn't unique or absent; (3) a running-counter safety net for any residual collision (fires for two unrelated repeated series that coincidentally share both a base name and a container index — observed live on OrangeHRM, see below). `id` is rebuilt from the final name so the HealStore/SmartLocator key stays unique too. `PomGenerator.generatePage()` also gained a defensive `assertUniqueNames()` guard that throws rather than silently emitting invalid TypeScript if this invariant is ever violated by a future change. Verified live: re-crawled SauceDemo (zero duplicate names/ids across all 4 pages, confirmed programmatically) and regenerated its `generated/` directory — `npm run check` now passes with zero errors (previously blocked by the duplicate-identifier compile error this entry describes). The stale `**/dashboard**` fixture bug noted in Session 9's scope check is also gone in the regenerated output (`fixtures.generated.ts` now correctly waits on `**/inventory.html**`). Also re-crawled OrangeHRM as a generalization check (not just SauceDemo) and found it had an **unlogged parallel instance of the same bug**, more severe: `viewEmployeeList` alone had 29 duplicate-name pairs (e.g. `employeeRow1Checkbox` x2), masked only because TD-019 separately blocks that app's full generation. Confirmed zero duplicate names/ids across all 21 OrangeHRM pages post-fix; tier 3's running-counter safety net fired for real there (OrangeHRM appears to render parallel desktop/mobile DOM copies of its tables, producing two independent repeated-row series with colliding base names *and* colliding container indices) — direct live validation that the safety net is needed, not just theoretical. Restful Booker (`rest-api`) is unaffected — `classifyPage()` short-circuits to the separate `classifyEndpoints()` static method before ever reaching DOM harvesting. **Found and fixed in the same change, not pre-existing:** the new `harvestElements()` helper functions initially used named `const`/`function` bindings, which broke immediately with `ReferenceError: __name is not defined` inside the browser — `tsx` hardcodes esbuild's `keepNames: true`, which wraps any named function with a `__name(...)` call living at the top of the *Node*-side compiled module, not in the string `page.evaluate()` ships to the browser via `fn.toString()`. Fixed by binding each helper via array-destructuring (`const [signature] = [(el) => ...]`), which gets no inferred name in real JS and so nothing for esbuild to wrap — confirmed empirically with an isolated repro before applying it. Logged as a new "common bug pattern" in `CLAUDE.md` since any future `page.evaluate()` helper in this codebase will hit it again. **Not fixed, explicitly out of scope (flagged, not silently absorbed):** regenerating SauceDemo's `generated/` directory for the first time also exposed TD-016 and TD-017 as **live, confirmed** blockers (previously only documented from static analysis) — all 5 freshly-generated SauceDemo tests fail on a double-login pattern, not a TD-018 symptom. See updated TD-016/TD-017 entries above. |
