Retired and replaced by an observation-only login-surface surface (LoginSurfaceObservation.ts) that records observations and draws no conclusion. This row's history is unchanged; the annotation records the downstream retirement. |
| TD-142 | **SSO/redirect login pages observe as authType 'none'.** The shipped authType detector's vocabulary is form-login/none (Bootstrap.detectAuthType — password-field presence). An SSO/redirect login page presents no password field and is observed as 'none', indistinguishable from a genuinely unauthenticated page. Consequence: a config declaring an SSO/oauth flow can never produce a meaningful authType comparison — observed 'none' vs configured 'oauth' currently yields 'divergence-detected' where 'inconclusive' would be the honest state (the detector's vocabulary cannot represent the configured flow, so it cannot actually confirm or deny divergence). Surfaced by TD-UI-027 deviation 2 (D1 tests a literal 'sso' value the detector cannot emit). | Medium | Needs a design conversation, not a patch: does the detector gain an SSO-redirect signal, or must authType comparison return 'inconclusive' when the configured flow is one the detector's vocabulary cannot represent? **Nova ruling required — honesty-floor question, not a feature gap.** NOT tagged · NB: until ruled, this is a potential false-positive in TD-UI-027's shipped authType signal, so its blocking status for the Onboard area is Aiden+Nova's call, not assumed non-blocking.  **RESOLVED (2026-07-18) — ADR-019 (Vocabulary Competence Boundary). The competence gate closes the false-positive: detectors now answer canRepresent(configuredValue); a comparison against a value outside the detector's vocabulary (configured 'sso'/'oauth' vs the authType detector's {form-login,none}) resolves to 'inconclusive' via a SECOND structural early return (sibling to the null-observation branch), and the CHECKED/NOT-CHECKED manifest names the limitation. New: docs/ADR/ADR-019_Vocabulary_Competence_Boundary.md; authTypeCanRepresent/appTypeCanRepresent (Bootstrap.ts); IdentityDivergence competence branch + dynamic manifest + partial-inconclusive remedy. D-a (KEY): sso-config + observed 'none' -> inconclusive, never no-divergence-detected. The CAPABILITY to actually detect SSO is follow-up TD-144 (own evaluation bar). D-a..D-e + D1-D8 green.** |

## Resolved — 2026-07-19 (ADR-019 amendment: discriminative competence)

| ID | Description | Priority-at-close | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-146 | **Identity-divergence baseUrl signal can report a FALSE divergence on redirect-based auth.** The probe navigates to baseUrl and records observed.baseUrl = page.url() (IdentityDivergence.ts ~:265-266). An SSO/redirect login sends the browser to an external IdP, so page.url() reports the IdP origin. The baseUrl comparison is NOT competence-gated — baseUrlCanRepresent (~:107) returns unconditional true — so the comparison proceeds and can emit 'divergence-detected' with observed=<IdP-origin> vs configured=<app-origin>, plus a tier-2 "re-onboard to re-detect application identity" remedy. Nothing about the configuration changed; the app redirected. The observation cannot distinguish "the app's base URL changed" from "the app redirected to an identity provider" — emitting a definitive outcome from an observation that cannot separate those hypotheses is exactly the ADR-019 defect class, the same class TD-142 closed for authType, arriving on the sibling signal that was not gated. **Provenance (honest):** this is in code shipped by commits ad28832/1b453c7 as part of the ADR-019 work itself; found by the 2026-07-18 EOD audit (SWEEP A, finding A1). Aiden's diff review accepted baseUrlCanRepresent's unconditional true with a clarifying comment rather than questioning whether it was true for the right reason. | High | **Onboard's blocking TD — Onboard is NOT green-eligible while this is open.** False positive in a shipped honesty surface. Design conversation required before code (Nova ruling pending); do not fix inline. Related: TD-142 / TD-144 (SSO), ADR-019 (competence boundary).  **RESOLVED (2026-07-19) — ADR-019 amended with discriminative competence (axis 2). baseUrl now resolves through the SAME structural BRANCH 2 as authType: baseUrlCompetence returns the axis-2 why when the observed origin differs from configured (under-determined by base-URL-change / SSO redirect / reverse proxy / maintenance / geo-tenant routing), so the comparison is inconclusive, never divergence-detected; only a matching origin concludes no-divergence. Manifest moves baseUrl to notChecked; tier-3 remedy names the limitation and never instructs re-onboard on this signal. New: ADR-019 amendment + baseUrlCompetence/discriminativeWhy (IdentityDivergence.ts); the false always-true comment removed. D-f (KEY) / D-g / D-h green; D1-D6/D8/D-a..D-e unchanged. Follow-ups: TD-147 (navigation-chain capability), TD-148 (focused axis-2 audit). CAVEAT at resolution: existing test D7 asserted the pre-fix baseUrl-divergence behavior and now correctly fails — its assertion must move to inconclusive (Aiden ruling pending; per the D-test guard the fix did NOT modify it inline).** |

## Resolved — 2026-07-19 (TD-148: identity-divergence comparison retired)

(Onboard identity-divergence surface retired to observation-only. Rows moved verbatim from Open; resolution evidence appended in **bold**. No row deleted.)

| ID | Description | Priority-at-close | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| TD-149 | **authType observation is under-determined — LIVE false positive on SPA logins.** The identity probe observes authType at IdentityDivergence.ts:298 via detectAuthType(page) using the DEFAULT immediate-count settling policy (Bootstrap.ts:171-179), NOT the SPA-aware policy Bootstrap.detect() uses. TD-110 documents that an SPA login form rendered after domcontentloaded reads as zero password fields -> observed 'none'. Observed 'none' is consistent with at least FIVE materially different causes: a failed/partial page load; a WAF/bot-wall/interstitial; a JS-rendered login form not yet present at count time; an already-authenticated session; an SSO redirect. Configured 'form-login' vs an under-determined 'none' yields divergence-detected + a tier-2 "re-onboard to re-detect application identity" remedy. Live false positive on the most common target shape (SPA), in the same surface TD-142/TD-146 corrected. Axis 1 does NOT save it — 'none' is inside the vocabulary, so the axis-1 gate lets the comparison proceed. | High | ADR-019 axis 2; never asked the axis-2 question. Design conversation required; do not patch. **Found by the TD-148 Onboard-slice audit, 2026-07-19, against a3ca29e. Shared suspected SINGLE root cause with TD-150 / TD-151: the probe fires AFTER auth failure and therefore observes a PRE-AUTH page, then draws conclusions about the application behind it. Nova ruling pending on whether these are three signal-level fixes or one root-cause fix; do NOT begin remediation on any of them until that ruling lands.**  **RESOLVED by capability retirement (2026-07-19), NOT by fix. The identity-divergence comparison this under-determination defeated is NO LONGER MADE — TD-148 retired it (root cause: observation domain != conclusion domain; the probe observed a pre-auth login surface and concluded about the application behind it) and replaced it with an observation-only login-surface surface (src/core/onboarding/LoginSurfaceObservation.ts) that draws no conclusion. Resolved because the claim it defeated is retired, not because the observation was made discriminative.** |
| TD-150 | **appType observation classifies the LOGIN page, not the application.** IdentityDivergence.ts:303 observes appType by classifying the pre-auth landing page (fetched :290, waitUntil 'domcontentloaded'). The same observed class arises from: a genuine appType; a statically-served login shell in front of an SPA; an SPA not finished hydrating at observation time; a maintenance/interstitial page classified on its own merits; pre-auth vs post-auth rendering differences. A definitive outcome is not uniquely warranted. | Medium | ADR-019 axis 2; never asked the axis-2 question. Resolved because the claim it defeated is retired, not because the observation was made discriminative.** |
| TD-151 | **baseUrl gate misses the SAME-ORIGIN under-determination.** The axis-2 gate added by TD-146 (baseUrlCompetence, IdentityDivergence.ts:131) covers origin DIFFERENCE. It does not cover same-origin under-determination: a same-origin error, maintenance, or tenant-router page satisfies sameOrigin() and yields no-divergence-detected, though the observation does not evidence that the configured application responded. Partial mitigation (record honestly): the manifest labels the signal "baseUrl (origin only)", scoping the claim to origin-match — under that narrow reading the outcome is warranted; the gap bites only where a consumer reads no-divergence-detected as "the configured app is what responded."
